# Skills · A · part 106

776 entries, am-i-covered-deductible-calculator to amazon-new-product-validation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **am-i-covered-deductible-calculator**: Use this when a policy shows a percentage deductible (hurricane, named storm, windstorm/hail, earthquake) or several deductibles, or when a user asks "how much would I pay out of pocket?" or "what is my hurricane deductible in dollars?" ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/am-i-covered/skills/am-i-covered-deductible-calculator) · ★6)
- **am-i-covered-florida-home**: Use this for any home or business property document (update-years prompt), and especially for Florida homes: wind mitigation inspection and credits, the My Safe Florida Home program, flood-only policies in Florida, and 4-point inspections for older homes. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/am-i-covered/skills/am-i-covered-florida-home) · ★6)
- **am-i-covered-getting-started**: Use this at the start of a new Am I Covered? chat, or when the user says "hi", "how does this work?", "get started", or "hola", before any document has been uploaded. - **am-i-covered-renewal-and-life-changes**: Use this when a user uploads two versions of the same policy (last term and this term, or a renewal notice), asks "what changed at renewal?" or "why did my premium go up?", or mentions a life change that could affect their insurance (new car, teen driver, renovation, pool, trampoline, dog, home business, moving, marriage, renting a room). ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/am-i-covered/skills/am-i-covered-renewal-and-life-changes) · ★6)

([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/am-i-covered/skills/am-i-covered-spanish) · ★6)
- **am-i-covered-summary-and-seasonal**: Use this when a user asks for a one-page summary, a cheat sheet, something to save or print, "sum up my coverage" (or "resumen de una página"), or a checkup before hurricane, winter/freeze, wildfire, or spring storm/hail season. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/am-i-covered/skills/am-i-covered-summary-and-seasonal) · ★6)
- **am-i-drifting**: Compare how someone writes now against how they used to write, and name what has changed. Use when someone asks whether they still sound like themselves, whether their posts have got worse or blander, whether AI has flattened their writing, or wants their recent posts compared with their older ones. Do not use it to compare one person against another person. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/prateeks367/voicemoat-skills/skills/am-i-drifting) · ★1.6k · 4 copies)
- **am-i-genius**: Assess how original an idea is through external research across products, companies, open source, papers, patents, APIs, extensions, communities, historical attempts, and adjacent solutions. Use only when the requested result is a sourced novelty verdict or comparison with existing work—whether the idea already exists, how novel it is, who has tried it, or what the closest precedents are. Search beyond the user's exact wording, synthesize the remaining gap, and state research limits. Originality and quality are separate questions. ([IamShushan/skills-ai-made-me-write](https://github.com/IamShushan/skills-ai-made-me-write/tree/HEAD/skills/am-i-genius) · ★1)

Use when the user asks "am I nerfed", whether they are in an experiment or A/B test, what x-cc-atis or an experiment ID means, or why Claude Code suddenly behaves differently. - **am-i-the-singularity**: Re-run the author's self-assessment of Claude against a fresh Claude in a sub-agent, or sit second chair while the reader runs it themselves. The author's questions, rules and turns from the recorded session of 1 October 2026 are put to a model that has not seen the answers; each answer is set beside the recorded one; the primary sources are opened in the library as they come up. Use when someone asks "would Claude say that again?", "let me try that conversation myself", "am I the Singularity?", "re-run the assessment", "cross-examine Claude", or doubts a recorded verdict.
